### greet-zh

```

  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 伙伴。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试。
```

```

```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPとUDPの一番の違いは、**TCPは届いたかを確認しながら順番どおり確実に届ける通信方式、UDPは確認や順番の管理をせず、速さを優先して送りっぱなしにする通信方式**という点です。

Webや메ール、ファイル転送のように正確さが大事な場面ではTCP、動画配信やオンラインゲームのように遅延を減らしたい場面ではUDPがよく使われます。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa **rebase** para ordenar tus commits locales y limpiar tu rama antes de compartirla. Usa **merge** para integrar una rama que ya se compartió, porque conserva el historial real y no reescribe commits que otros pueden tener.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠、有序、面向连接的传输，保证数据送达且顺序正确；UDP 不保证这些，只管尽快发出去，速度快、开销小。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own unshared branch to replay your commits on top of the latest base and keep history linear.
Merge when the branch is shared or already pushed, because rebasing rewrites commits others may have based work on.
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 用文件路径就行，直接跟在 `bun test` 后面：

```bash
bun test tests/foo.test.ts
```

Bun 会把参数当作路径过滤，所以写目录或文件名的一部分也能匹配，比如 `bun test foo` 会跑所有路径里带 `foo` 的测试文件。

如果只想跑某个测试用例，用 `-t`（或 `--test-name-pattern`）按名字过滤：

```bash
bun test tests/foo.test.ts -t "登录失败"
```
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log records every change to durable storage before the change touches the main data files. After a crash, the database replays that log to restore any committed work that hadn't reached the data files yet.
```

|---|---|---|

| Memory | 16 GB | 16 GB |
| Power | Always plugged in | Battery, so it needs to stay on the charger |
| Lid or sleep | No lid, so nothing to close | Closing the lid sleeps it unless you change settings |
| Cooling | Fan keeps it running hard all day | Fanless, which is quiet but can throttle under long loads |
| Ethernet | Built in | Needs a Wi-Fi connection or an adapter |
| Ports | More, including HDMI | Fewer |
| Portability | Stays put | Comes with you |

The Air only wins if you want one machine that moves with you and also runs me at home. For an always-on home, the mini is the better buy for $400 less.
```

});
ducter.onChange(function () {

      render({ trains: v, updated: u });
    });
  });
});

  document.getElementById('add').addEventListener('click', function(){
    var w = parseFloat(document.getElementById('nw').value);
    var p = parseFloat(document.getElementById('np').value);
    var s = parseFloat(document.getElementById('ns').value);
    if (isNaN(w) || isNaN(p)) return;
    nights().then(function(list){
      list.push({night: list.length + 1, writes: w, p99: p, stalls: isNaN(s) ? 0 : s});
      return Promise.resolve(ducter.set('nights', list)).then(load);
    });
    ['nw','np','ns'].forEach(function(id){ document.getElementById(id).value = ''; });
  });
  if (typeof ducter.onChange === 'function') ducter.onChange(load);
  load();
})();

const esc = s => String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const pad = n => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
const WD = '日一二三四五六';
let state = { days: ['2026-11-23'], expenses: [] };
let selDay = null, openId = null;

function money(n) {
  return '¥' + (Math.round(n * 100) / 100).toLocaleString('zh-CN', {  … st('[data-d]');
  if (chip) { selDay = chip.dataset.d; render(); return; }

  const del = e.target.closest('[data-del]');
  if (del) {
    state.expenses = state.expenses.filter(x => x.id !== del.dataset.del);
    openId = null;
    await save(); render(); return;
  }

  const row = e.target.closest('[data-row]');
  if (row) { openId = openId === row.dataset.row ? null : row.dataset.row; render(); return; }

  if (e.target.id === 'addday') {
    const last = state.days[state.days.length - 1];
    const [y, m, dd] = last.split('-').map(Number);
    const nd = new Date(y, m - 1, dd + 1);
    const s = `${nd.getFullYear()}-${pad(nd.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad(nd.getDate())}`;
    state.days.push(s);
    selDay = s;
    await save(); render(); return;
  }

  if (e.target.id === 'save') {
    const amt = parseFloat($('amt').value);
    if (!(amt > 0)) { $('amt').focus(); return; }
    const item = $('item').value.trim() || '未填';
    state.expenses.push({ id: Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6), day: selDay, item, amount: amt });
    $('item').value = '';
    $('amt').value = '';
    await save(); render();
  }
});

ducter.onChange(() => load());
load();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}

}

      lab.querySelector('.k-row-main').textContent = it.title;
      lab.querySelector('.k-row-sub').textContent = it.sub;
      (it.draft ? d : w).appendChild(lab);
      if (!it.draft && !done[it.id]) n++;
    });
    document.getElementById('count').textContent = n;
    if (window.ducter && ducter.summary) ducter.summary(n + ' waiting');
  }
  document.addEventListener('change', function(e){
    if (!e.target.classList.contains('k-tick')) return;
    done[e.target.dataset.id] = e.target.checked;
    draw();
    if (window.ducter && ducter.set) ducter.set('done', done);
  });
  function load(){
    return Promise.all([
      Promise.resolve(ducter.get('items')),
      Promise.resolve(ducter.get('done'))
    ]).then(function(r){
      items = Array.isArray(r[0]) ? r[0] : [];
      done = (r[1] && typeof r[1] === 'object') ? r[1] : {};
      draw();
    });
  }
  load();
  if (window.ducter && ducter.onChange) ducter.onChange(load);
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"id":"ducter-412","title":"wey-gu/ducter #412 · compaction cut","sub":"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Monday"},{"id":"mem-10287","title":"nowledge-co/mem #10287 · retry on 502","sub":"CI is failing"},{"id":"hawdb-1592","title":"nowledge-co/hawdb #1592 · flaky compaction test","sub":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday"},{"id":"ducter-418","title":"wey-gu/ducter #418 · apps bar","sub":"Still a draft","draft":true}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">出差花销合计</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="total"></div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub"></div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">花销明细</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
<details class="k-add"><summary>记一笔</summary>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-field"><label>出差名称</label><input class="k-input" id="trip"></div>
    <div class="k-field"><label>日期</label><input class="k-input" id="day" placeholder="2026-11-20"></div>
    <div class="k-field"><label>分类</label>
      <div class="k-chips" id="cats">
        <button class="k-chip" data-c="交通" aria-pressed="true">交通</button>
        <button class="k-chip" data-c="住宿" aria-pressed="false">住宿</button>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="k-field"><label>金额</label><input class="k-input k-num" id="amt" inputmode="decimal"></div>
    <button class="k-btn primary" id="save">保存</button>
  </div>
</details>
</main>
<script>
let cat="交通";
function esc(s){return String(s??"").replace(/[&<>"]/g,c=>({"&":"&amp;","<":"&lt;",">":"&gt;",'"':"&quot;"}[c]))}
function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("items")||[];
  const total=xs.reduce((a,x)=>a+Number(x …  document.getElementById("sub").textContent=xs.length?xs.length+" 笔":"记下第一笔花销后这里显示合计";
  const list=document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML=xs.length?xs.map(x=>'<div class="k-row"><div class="k-row-main">'+esc(x.trip)+' · '+esc(x.cat)+'</div><div class="k-row-sub">'+esc(x.day)+(x.note?' · '+esc(x.note):'')+'</div><div class="k-row-value">¥'+Number(x.amt||0).toLocaleString("zh-CN")+'</div></div>').join(""):'<div class="k-empty">还没有花销，点下面的「记一笔」开始。</div>';
  const xsSum=total;
  ducter.summary(xs.length?xs.length+" 笔,合计 ¥"+xsSum.toLocaleString("zh-CN"):"还没记");
}
document.querySelectorAll("#cats .k-chip").forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{
  cat=b.dataset.c;
  document.querySelectorAll("#cats .k-chip").forEach(o=>o.setAttribute("aria-pressed",o===b?"true":"false"));
});
document.getElementById("save").onclick=()=>{
  const trip=document.getElementById("trip").value.trim();
  const amt=Number(document.getElementById("amt").value);
  if(!trip||!amt)return;
  const xs=ducter.get("items")||[];
  xs.push({trip,day:document.getElementById("day").value.trim(),cat,amt});
  ducter.set("items",xs);
  document.getElementById("amt").value="";
  draw();
};
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"2026-11-20","cat":"交通","amt":662,"note":"G1 上海虹桥→北京南"},{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"2026-11-20","cat":"住宿","amt":1036,"note":"亚朵酒店（望京）两晚"}]}

### picture-question-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 图里这是什么？适合放在空状态里吗？ (with a picture: dev_laptop_awake.webp)
  [partner message] 这是一台卡通风格的 3D 笔记本电脑：奶油米色机身，深蓝黑色的屏幕边框，屏幕是空白的，键盘上有按键细节。

很适合放在空状态里。屏幕空白正好表达"还没有内容"，圆润柔和的造型也比较友好，不会让人觉得冷冰冰。

用的时候有几点要注意：
- 尺寸偏小（320×246），空状态里显示在 120 到 160 像素左右比较合适，别放太大。
- 图本身没有文字，最好配一句说明和一个按钮，比如"还没有记录，点这里开始"。
- 看起来是白底。放到其他底色上之前，先确认它是不是透明背景。
- 深色模式下米色会显得很亮，可能需要换一个暗色版本，或者把图稍微调暗。
```
